===== [p181-181] 5 Thermocouple and resistance temperature sensor replacement procedure (for processing bath) > How to handle the replaced parts (1块) =====
--[p181]-- How to handle the replaced parts
The replaced parts should be disposed of as industrial wastes.
(1) Rinse the replaced parts to eliminate all remaining chemical.
(2) Dry the parts.
(3) Securely pack the parts so that any chemical remaining inside will not leak out.
(4) Write down the parts names, materials and chemicals used.
(5) If special ventilation is necessary for safety, request that a qualified specialist handle the parts as industrial wastes.
In cases where storage is necessary, keep them in a designated safe place.
Thermocouple replacement procedure (at exit of inline heater)

--[p115]-- Chemical valve
1) After aligning the lever to the closed position, close the cover and lock it.
System startup

===== [p23-40] WARNING (12块) =====
--[p38]-- WARNING
• Before starting maintenance, shut off supply of power and all other utilities to the equipment, and make sure that no pressure remains in the equipment. If it is necessary to perform maintenance while utilities are being supplied to the equipment, ensure the safety of the engineers.
• Before performing maintenance on the electrical lines, shut off all power being supplied to the equipment.
--[p40]-- Even when disposing of the water that was used for the cleaning, engineers should keep their protective gear on and use the prescribed procedures and locations.
Clean the protective gear and containers as well, and return them to their storage locations.
Restore the workspaces to their original states.
7. Concluding meeting (Confirmation checks after finishing maintenance)
1) Confirm that all the procedures have been completed without any problems.
2) Check whether there is any other information to be confirmed.
--[p38]-- In addition, close the source valves for DI water, cooling water, and city water, as necessary. In this case, attach a tag to each valve, clearly indicating that an adjustment is underway, in order to prevent anyone other than the associated maintenance engineers from inadvertently initiating supply of power, gases, DI water, or chemicals to the equipment.
--[p23]-- WARNING
• Even after power to the equipment has been turned OFF, some parts and areas in the equipment remain at a high temperature. Take care to avoid contact with these high temperature parts and areas. Also note that chemical solutions continue to be fed to the chemical processing modules. Be extremely careful not to touch the chemicals or breathe in gas or vapor.
• Even after the emergency stop switch is activated, some areas continue to carry dangerous electrical current.
Never touch the power supply areas.
--[p39]-- In particular, if it is necessary to perform maintenance during operation of the equipment, engineers should give verbal confirmations to one another and exercise caution regarding the movement of the drive section.
6) If leakage droplets are found
If you find droplets in your workspace, check the pH with pH paper and remove the droplets with a clean wiper.
--[p39]-- WARNING
• When loosening the joint sections of the chemical piping, be sure not to splash chemical from the piping.
• When two or more maintenance engineers are involved in the maintenance procedure, they should check the maintenance procedure being performed by the other engineer(s), as well as the overall condition of the equipment.
--[p40]-- WARNING
Strictly follow the specified recovery procedures. Inappropriate recovery procedures may lead to an accident.
8) Perform cleaning and disposal procedures.
After maintenance is completed, be sure to clean and re-organize the surrounding areas. Clean the wipers, gloves, and protective sheets that were used to perform maintenance in the chemical sections, and dispose of them according to the prescribed disposal procedures and locations.
--[p38]-- WARNING
If the exhaust system is turned OFF or decreased, chemical gases or vapors in the processing baths may leak from the equipment, which can adversely affect personnel. A similar situation may occur if the butterfly valve or the slit of the exhaust section is closed or the exhaust scrubber on the primary side (main factory system) is stopped. Note that electricity is still supplied to the primary side even after the main circuit breaker has been turned OFF.
--[p38]-- If necessary, turn OFF the power supply for the factory’s facility system.
5. Precautions to be taken during the procedures
1) Strictly forbid handling of ignition sources.
Never allow open flames, spark sources, etc. in the vicinity of the equipment. Learn the safety standards for handling ignition sources from the safety manager of the clean room, and follow the instructions during the maintenance procedure.
2) Wear protective gear during maintenance on the chemical sections.
--[p39]-- For droplets determined as not being neutral with pH paper, eliminate them with a waterdampened clean wiper and wipe clean the area of the droplets until it is neutralized.
--[p38]-- If it is necessary to perform maintenance on the electrical lines while power is being supplied to the equipment, make sure to wear the appropriate protective gear, including the rubber gloves.
• Even after the power has been shut off, there are some sections that continue to carry high voltage. After the power has been shut off, check the safety using a tester before starting maintenance.
4) Turn OFF the main circuit breaker and the chemical valves.
--[p38]-- When performing maintenance on the chemical sections, wear appropriate protective goggles and mask, and chemical resistant apron, gloves, and arm covers.

===== [p197-201] 12 Automatic cover opening/closing speed adjustment procedure > Preparation (3块) =====
--[p197]-- Preparation
(1) Confirm that no lots exist in the equipment and switch the equipment mode to manual.
(2) Using remote control, move the transfer unit (ROB) to any module except for the one being adjusted. Then, disconnect the remote control.
(3) Turn OFF the power to the transfer unit. The lock-out key must be kept by the manager.
(4) Remove the front lower cover of the module to be replaced.
--[p200]-- Preparation
(1) Confirm that no lots exist in the equipment and switch the equipment mode to manual.
(2) To prevent adhesion of chemicals, clean the chemical bath at least three times. Concerning the rinsing bath, perform DI water upflow for 5 minutes or more.
(3) Flush the piping where leaking has occurred with DI water. The pressure must not be applied at the ambient temperature.
(4) Close the DI water and chemical source valves.
(5) Turn the equipment power OFF and then lock out the equipment in accordance with the lockout procedure.
--[p201]-- Preparation
(1) Confirm that no lots exist in the equipment and switch the equipment mode to manual.
(2) Using the overhead operation panel, open the automatic cover of the spin dryer.
(3) Turn OFF the equipment power. The key must be kept by the manager.
(4) Open the sliding cover located at the front of the main body.

===== [p113-113] WARNING > 2.3 To shut down the equipment for a long period: (3块) =====
--[p113]-- 2.3 To shut down the equipment for a long period:
1) Select the [Parameter] and then the [Maintenance Parameter] menus. Set “Chemical control” for all the chemical baths to “0” (OFF) and drain each processing bath.
2) Close the supply valves (toggle valves) to the measuring tanks, and empty them by transferring their contents to the processing baths.
3) Execute bath cleaning for all the chemical processing baths.
4) Drain and empty all the processing baths.
5) Exit “Windows NT”.
--[p113]-- • To cool the heaters down, power will continue to be supplied to all modules for about one minute after the “MAIN POWER” switch is turned OFF.
• Always shut off the uninterrupted power supply before turning off the primary side power supply. Otherwise, electrical discharges can damage the uninterrupted power supply.
--[p113]-- (Refer to step 6 in “2.2 To shut down the equipment for a short period:”.)
6) Turn off the switches for “MAIN POWER”, “ROBOT POWER”and “LIGHT”.
7) Turn off the uninterrupted power supply.
8) Turn off the primary side circuit breaker at the distribution board.
9) Cut off the supply of utilities if necessary.
NOTE
• Do not turn off the exhaust even for a long-term shut-down.
• Filters may dry out during long-term shut-down.

===== [p23-118] WARNING > WARNING (12块) =====
--[p114]-- WARNING
Confirm that the cover interlocks are set to active.
1) If any alarm related to the transfer system is activated, clear only that particular alarm.
2) After the preparations for chemical system have been completed, confirm that the cover interlocks are set to active, set the remote control key to invalid, and then execute a home return of the transfer system.
System shutdown
--[p28]-- WARNING
This operation must be performed only by an authorized employee. Do not allow ordinary operators to perform this operation.
--[p110]-- Chemical collection must be carried out for each chemical and must never be done for more than one chemical at a time.
• If the cover interlocks are overridden, the interlocks will not work when they are necessary, which increases the possibility of a dangerous incident, such as being caught in a moving section or a serious accident due to the scattering of chemicals/fumes.
--[p37]-- • Before collecting (for recycling) or draining the chemical or DI water, confirm that the factory’s recycling or drainage facility is ready. Carefully check, in particular, the facility requirements for the given chemical type (faulty mixture of chemicals), amount of drainage (overflow), and the valve open/closed state.
Before performing maintenance procedures
--[p36]-- WARNING
Maintenance procedures must be performed by specially trained and authorized maintenance engineers. Do not allow operators to perform maintenance.
--[p118]-- WARNING
During a home return operation, keep all parts of your body well away from the moving sections.
When the home return operation of each moving section has been completed, the lamp of HOME key ceases blinking and turns on.
6) If the cover for any bath did not open, check again to see if any lots are left in that particular bath. After checking, be sure to close the cover or lower the lifter platform. If this instruction is not observed, processing cannot be started in the automatic mode.
--[p110]-- WARNING
• Before collecting (for recycling) or draining chemical or DI water, first confirm that your company’s recycling or drainage facility is ready. Carefully check, in particular, the facility requirements for the given chemical type, amount of drainage, and the valve open/close state.
• Chemical collection involves the danger of a chemical reaction that may result in unexpected problems.
--[p116]-- WARNING
Before starting up the utility systems, confirm that the exhaust system and the clean unit work properly. For information on the correct exhaust amount and pressure, refer to the inspection sheets.
1) Open all of the DI water source valve(s), pneumatic valves, and $\Nu _ { 2 }$ valves for the equipment.

• This operation must be performed only by authorized personnel. Do not allow ordinary operators to perform this operation.

When restarting the operation of the equipment, confirm that the cause of the emergency stop has been resolved.
Equipment safety information
--[p37]-- Thus, it is necessary to perform the following preparations before beginning maintenance on a chemical supply system: First, put on appropriate protective gear (protective goggles, chemical resistant gloves, mask, etc.), next drain the chemical from the piping and processing baths, and then wash them thoroughly. In addition, use a portable exhaust unit whenever necessary.
--[p37]-- WARNING
• Use extreme care when removing chemical section covers on which chemical may remain. After cleaning a gangway or baffle disk, remove water with a clean wiper before positioning it in place.
• Toxic, corrosive, and flammable chemicals are used in this equipment.
--[p26]-- WARNING
This operation must be performed only by authorized personnel. Do not allow ordinary operators to perform this operation.   
If any leakage is found, do not operate the equipment until appropriate measures (i.e., repair) have been taken.

===== [p150-153] 6.3 LPD-IPA temperature control bath level sensor check Equipment mode > Inspection procedure (5块) =====
--[p152]-- Inspection procedure
(1) From the overhead operation panel, close the LPD auto cover.
(2) From the overhead operation panel, move the LPD lifter to its upper position.

===== [p37-37] WARNING > 3. Person in charge (4块) =====
--[p37]-- 3) Clean the chemical sections.
Perform the following preparations prior to chemical section maintenance: First thoroughly drain the chemical from the bath/chamber, and then supply and circulate cleaning solutions (DI water, etc.) to clean the bath/chamber along with the connected piping lines and filters.
Use the hand shower if there is a section that cannot be sufficiently cleaned by the circulation function.
Remember to clean any other parts to which chemical may still adhere.
--[p37]-- 3. Person in charge
Prior to beginning maintenance, determine the person in charge of the maintenance work. The role of the person in charge is important for ensuring safe and reliable maintenance work.
• Assurance of safety
• Check of the maintenance procedures
• Confirmation of proper completion of the maintenance procedures
--[p37]-- The person in charge must precisely understand the work procedures and give clear instructions to all associated maintenance engineers, while taking charge of the emergency report in the event of an accident.
4. Preparations before beginning the procedures (Workspace arrangement and stop of utility supply to the equipment)
1) Secure enough workspace for each procedure.
--[p37]-- Set cone bars to distinguish each workspace from the surrounding area, in order to prevent access by anyone other than the specified maintenance engineers or accidents due to the OHT or OHV.
2) Secure a space for temporary placement of parts and covers removed during the maintenance work.
Set appropriate protective sheets over the areas that may be subject to water or chemical splash, and secure them so they do not slip from their positions.
Take care to prevent dripping of liquid leaks.

===== [p52-52] WARNING > (4) Delay timer for designated level detection (3块) =====
--[p52]-- $\mathsf { H } _ { 3 } \mathsf { P O } _ { 4 }$ bath module | DI water measuringtank | ${ \mathrm { H } } _ { 3 } { \mathrm { P O } } _ { 4 }$ measuring tank
--[p52 [表]]-- (4) Delay timer for designated level detection
A delay timer is used to detect the specified level for solution supply. When the designated level sensor (level 5) is activated and remains on for a pre-set time (delay time), solution supply to the processing bath stops. The delay time should be set as a maintenance parameter (MP) for the IN-CTC in 0.1 seconds according to the size (capacity) of the measuring tank.

===== [p73-73] WARNING > 2.6.1 Chemical exchange sequence (4块) =====
--[p73]-- (iv) Updating lifetime and life count
The lifetime and the life count are reset to “0” when all chemical supply operations are completed.
(v) Waiting for chemical heat-up
When chemical supply is completed, chemical heating is started. When the chemical reaches the specified temperature range, temperature control is completed 2 minutes after the time set in the “Temp. check time” maintenance parameter.
When temperature control is completed, the chemical exchange sequence is also completed.
--[p73]-- 2.6.1 Chemical exchange sequence
(i) Chemical drainage
When bath cleaning is specified, the equipment also starts the chemical drainage process as part of the bath cleaning sequence.
(ii) Bath cleaning
The bath cleaning sequence starts. If bath cleaning is not specified, the process skips to the chemical supply process.
(iii) Chemical supply process
After confirming that the chemical measuring tank is completely refilled, the chemical is supplied according to the specified procedure.
--[p73]-- During chemical exchange from measuring tanks with a pre-heating function, chemical will be supplied even if the pre-heating has not been completed. Each measuring tank assumes that supply is completed when the empty level sensor turns OFF and the empty confirmation timer expires (60 seconds), and the heater turns on when all chemical supply operations are completed.
--[p73]-- The circulation pump starts operation when the designated level sensor in the outer bath turns ON during chemical supply, and stops operation when the lower limit level sensor in the outer bath is turned OFF by pump circulation.
If “Bath chemical upper level error,” “Pump/heater leak” or “N<sub>2</sub> low pressure error” occurs during a chemical supply operation, the operation stops. Chemical supply is resumed when the cause is corrected and the alarm is reset.

===== [p42-42] WARNING > 1.4.5 Dangers of working in high places (1块) =====
--[p42]-- 1.4.5 Dangers of working in high places
It is extremely dangerous to perform maintenance in high places because an engineer may stand on unsteady places from where they could fall due to a false step. In addition, they must be careful to prevent the inadvertent dropping of a tool or a part. If something may be dropped, it is necessary to take preliminary countermeasures to protect surrounding personnel and products against injuries or damage.

===== [p143-143] CAUTION > <If a leak is found:> (2块) =====
--[p143]-- <If a leak is found:>
• If the equipment is in the automatic mode, switch it to the manual mode and correct the cause of the leak.
• Flush the leak area using a hand shower to sufficiently dilute the chemical. Confirm the diluted state with litmus paper. Note, however, that litmus paper cannot be used with BHF because it is neutral. If there is a BHF leak, flush the area very carefully.
--[p143]-- • If there is a leak from a fitting, follow the procedure described in “Further tightening the fittings” in Chapter 6 “Maintenance Procedure” in this manual.

--[p34]-- 3.1.1 Precautions for disposal operations

2) As equipment with a UPS (uninterrupted power supply) unit installed uses a secondary battery that contains heavy metals (i.e., Pb, Ni, Cd, etc.), dispose of such equipment in compliance with the relevant laws and ordinances of your country.

===== [p131-131] CAUTION > 3 Safety during periodic inspection (7块) =====
--[p131]-- While performing inspections, do not allow any person to come near the equipment except for the operators in charge.
6. Disregarding an inspection procedure could result in physically contacting or inhaling chemicals or fumes. If a procedure cautions you to wear protective gear, most certainly follow the instruction before beginning inspections.
7.
--[p131]-- 3 Safety during periodic inspection
<Handling the parts/sections requesting periodic replacement or inspection> This manual only describes the periodic replacements or inspections to be performed by the customer. Parts or sections not described here could endanger the human body or degrade equipment performance if any procedural mistake is made.
--[p131]-- This equipment needs to undergo the inspection using special tools every six months or once a year to ensure the reliability of its processing and operations.
9. If any problem was found in the periodic inspections, report it to Dainippon Screen’s engineers unless otherwise instructed.
--[p131]-- Those maintenance engineers must thoroughly read and fully understand all the contents of the Operation Manual and Reference Manual before implementing the inspections and then strictly follow the procedures described in this manual.
2. Before collecting (for recycling) or draining chemical or DI water, first confirm that your company’s recycling or drainage facility is ready.
--[p131]-- (For details about these kind of parts, refer to the parts list.) When replacements or inspections of those parts/sections are necessary, request them from Dainippon Screen’s engineers.
1. The periodic inspections must not be performed by any personnel except specially trained and authorized maintenance engineers.
--[p131]-- Carefully check, in particular, the facility requirements for the given chemical type, amount of drainage, and the valve open/close state.
3. Inspections that require remote control operations must be performed by more than one person.
4. Before beginning inspections, post a display notice such as “Under inspection” to prevent other persons from inadvertently operating the equipment.
5.
--[p131]-- While the daily inspection is composed of visual checks during automatic operations, the three-month and semiannual inspections require remote control operations in the manual operation mode. To ensure safety, avoid overriding the cover interlocks.
8. Please request implementation of the semiannual or annual inspection from our nearest branch offices listed on the back page of this manual.

===== [p91-95] WARNING > Spin dry time (2块) =====
--[p91]-- Spin dry time
The processing time (including acceleration time during startup) should be set in seconds in the user parameters.
--[p95]-- Spin dry time
The processing time (including acceleration time during startup) should be set in seconds in the user parameters.
Acceleration time
Acceleration time to speed up motor rotation until it reaches the specified speed (in sec.).
(Maximum acceleration: 0 to 1000 rpm/10 sec.= 50 Hz)
Deceleration time
Deceleration time to slow down motor rotation before it stops (in sec.). The acceleration and deceleration times must be equal.
DC braking time
Time to brake the motor rotation after it is turned off.
Rotation speed (Frequency)
Low speed rotation (rpm) (Max. 1000 rpm = 50 Hz)
Dryer module

===== [p190-190] 9 Measuring tank immersion heater replacement procedure > Preparation (2块) =====
--[p190]-- Preparation
(1) Confirm that no lots exist in the equipment and switch the equipment mode to manual.
(2) Turn the temperature control switch OFF.

===== [p72-72] WARNING > 3) Automatic chemical exchange 2 (1块) =====
--[p72]-- 3) Automatic chemical exchange 2
When the chemical life expires in a bath, chemical exchange starts. However, if there are wafers being processed in the expired bath, chemical exchange starts only after the wafers are processed and discharged. If wafers to be processed in the expired bath are located in an upstream module or at the loader, the lot stands by at the (rinsing) module preceding the expired bath.
Chemical bath modules

--[p79]-- NOTE
For both pre-process and interval replenishment, the amount of chemical to be replenished is calculated by multiplying the amount output from the pump per shot (1 shot: 5 cc) and the number of replenishment pump shots (shot) specified in the parameters.

===== [p59-59] WARNING > (1) Flowchart (2块) =====
--[p59]-- This flowchart applies to equipment with standard specifications. Some parameters such as the Empty Check time differ according to equipment specifications.
Chemical bath modules
--[p59]-- (1) Flowchart
(b) Circulation is performed for the time specified in the “Pump delay time” maintenance parameter to cool down the heater.
(c) The aspirator (AS1) turns on and the drainage valves in the inner bath (DR5) and in the outer bath (DR4) open.
(d) When the empty confirmation times for the outer and inner baths (fixed at 60 seconds) expire, the pipe drainage valves (DR1, 2, 3) open.
(e) When drainage is completed, the drainage valves (DR1, 2, 3) close and the aspirator (AS1) stops.

--[p133]-- 4.2 DI water pressure check

===== [p186-186] 6 Thermocouple replacement procedure (at exit of inline heater) > How to handle the replaced parts (1块) =====
--[p186]-- How to handle the replaced parts
The replaced parts should be disposed of as industrial wastes.
(1) Rinse the replaced parts to eliminate all remaining chemical.
(2) Dry the parts.
(3) Securely pack the parts so that any chemical remaining inside will not leak out.
(4) Write down the parts names, materials and chemicals used.
(5) If special ventilation is necessary for safety, request that a qualified specialist handle the parts as industrial wastes.
In cases where storage is necessary, keep them in a designated safe place.
Circulation pump replacement procedure

===== [p121-177] CAUTION (3块) =====
--[p121]-- CAUTION
Be extremely careful when checking the interior of the processing bath line. Opening the front door or putting any part of your body inside the bath enclosures can cause serious burns and/or inhalation of toxic fumes.
--[p159]-- CAUTION
Wear appropriate protective gear, since there is the possibility that you will come in direct contact with chemicals.
--[p177]-- CAUTION
When replacing filters to be used with other chemicals, you can usually just substitute the chemical to be used for IPA in the hydrophilic processing example above and otherwise follow the same procedure.
However, it is dangerous to mix water directly highly concentrated sulfuric acid, so be careful to increase the concentration of acid very gradually.

===== [p138-138] CAUTION > Circulation section (1块) =====
--[p138]-- Circulation section
From the maintenance area, check the circulation pressure gauge in the circulation piping section and confirm that the reading is at the value or within the range specified on the valve notice plate.

===== [p200-218] 12 Automatic cover opening/closing speed adjustment procedure > NOTE (8块) =====
--[p210]-- NOTE
If the red LED of the leakage sensor is still lit after the restoration, the area from which the leakage occurred may have not been wiped off adequately. Wipe off this area again and reset the alarm.
--[p218]-- NOTE
The alarm reset operation must be performed after you have located the error and found the cause. Performing alarm reset without taking any measures to correct the error may cause damage to the lots and equipment. If the error correction was performed while a lot (or lots) remain(s) in the equipment, confirm that the lot(s) is(are) still located at the same position after error correction and reset the alarm.
--[p200]-- NOTE
If there is leakage even after the union nut is tightened until the gap between the union nut and the body reaches the specified lower limit value (measured with a thickness gauge), replace the fitting with a new one.
--[p210]-- NOTE
For more information concerning the usage of the manual switch (or remote control switch), refer to Chapter 8.
Countermeasures against leakage
8) Keep everyone other than the maintenance engineers who are wearing protective gear away from the equipment.
9) After confirming that no authorized person remains near the equipment, remove the piping rear cover of the leaking section.
10) With the DIW hand shower, rinse the leaking area for more than 5 minutes to thoroughly remove the chemical.
--[p210]-- NOTE
When the Cycle Pause switch is blinking, it indicates that the equipment’s mode is switching to the Cycle Pause mode, and when the switch turns ON, in indicates that the mode has changed to Cycle Pause.
3) Wear protective gear. All protective gear, such as gas mask, protective goggles, protective gloves and chemical protective apron, must be suitable for the leaking chemical.
4) Prepare a DIW hand shower and confirm that the shower hose is long enough to reach the leakage location.
5) Provide for a local exhaust using flexible exhaust piping or another method.
--[p210]-- NOTE
Confirm that the exhaust piping is long enough to reach the leakage location.
6) Confirm that the equipment is in the Cycle Pause state and press the MANUAL switch to change the operation mode to manual.
7) Drain the chemical in the module having the leakage, using the manual switch (or remote control switch).
--[p211]-- Be careful so that the rinse fluid does not overflow from the drainage line.
11) With pH test paper, confirm that there is no chemical remaining.
For BHF, which is neutral, pH test paper can not be used. Therefore, when there is a BHF leakage, rinse the leakage for more than 10 minutes continuously.
12) Wipe the wet area with clean cloth.
13) Perform any necessary repairs or adjustments, such as the replacement or tightening of parts or fittings.
NOTE
For details on the tightening of the fittings, refer to the Accessory Units/Components Instruction Manual.
--[p213]-- NOTE
If the error which brought the equipment to the CYCLE PAUSE state is defined as alarm level 4, the CYCLE PAUSE can be reset prior to error correction.
Troubleshooting

===== [p15-15] WS-820/MTR series manuals > 1.12.2 MSDS acquisition (1块) =====
--[p15]-- 1.12.2 MSDS acquisition
Users of this equipment must themselves obtain a copy of the MSDS for each gas from their suppliers.
Ensure that all personnel involved with this equipment clearly understand the properties, potential dangers and proper handling of gases being used with the equipment based on the MSDSs obtained from the suppliers.

===== [p177-177] CAUTION > Operation check procedure (3块) =====
--[p177]-- 3) When the circulation pump is working (i.e., the filter is being cleaned) during the bath cleaning, measure the circulation pressure with the pressure gauge. If the circulation pressure is remarkably higher than usual, the filter is not hydrophilic enough. Soak the filter with IPA (IPA replacement) again, and then restart the operation check from step 1.
--[p177]-- Operation check procedure
1) Perform bath cleaning using the overhead operation panel. Keep the parameter setting values changed during steps 1 through 3 of the “Work procedure”.
2) Without removing the cover, visually confirm during the bath cleaning that the no leakage occurs around the filter unit. If you find any leakage, stop the bath cleaning and further tighten the part causing the leakage. Then restart the operation check from step 1.
--[p177]-- 4) After the bath cleaning is completed, remove the maintenance cover for the piping section and confirm that no leakage occurs around the filter unit.
5) Re-attach the maintenance cover for the piping section and clear all the currently issued alarms.
6) Restore the parameters you changed during step 1 through 3 with the previous values recorded.
7) Perform chemical exchange using the overhead operation panel.

===== [p81-81] WARNING > 2.10.1 Orifice method (1块) =====
--[p81]-- 2.10.1 Orifice method
To vent air trapped in the filter, a pipe has been installed connecting the filter vent to the outer processing bath. A fixed diameter orifice has been opened in the piping which vents air and a small quantity of chemical during circulation.

===== [p42-42] WARNING > 1.4.6 Dangers of carrying heavy objects (1块) =====
--[p42]-- 1.4.6 Dangers of carrying heavy objects
Due caution needs to be exercised when moving parts or objects that are heavy or difficult to carry.
According to the need, entrust the transportation to a special contractor.
1. Avoid trying to carry heavy objects that are beyond your capacity.
2. Check the surrounding circumstances to see if there are any obstacles.
3. When multiple engineers are carrying an object together, they should pay attention to one another to maintain the balance of the object.
4. To carry a heavy object in a high place, use an appropriate device such as a hand lifter.

===== [p249-249] 12 Automatic cover opening/closing speed adjustment procedure > 2 Operations related to lot deletion (1块) =====
--[p249]-- 2 Operations related to lot deletion
The system is always monitoring the status data for lots currently in the equipment such as their lot IDs and current lot positions. The lot data can be checked in the [Status Display] window or the [Lot Tracking] window.
Therefore, if a lot which has been loaded needs to be removed for any reason, the lot data also must be deleted.

===== [p88-88] WARNING > 3.3.2 When a resistivity error occurs: (1块) =====
--[p88]-- 3.3.2 When a resistivity error occurs:
When a resistivity error occurs while wafer processing is nearing completion in the FR bath module, upflow continues and an alarm is generated. Correct the resistivity error as follows.
1) Press “BUZZ OFF” to stop the buzzer sound.
2) Upflow continues and the wafers are automatically discharged when the resistivity recovers.
3) The alarm on the display disappears when the alarm is reset on the main operation panel after the wafer discharge.

===== [p78-78] WARNING > 2.8.2 Pre-process replenishment (1块) =====
--[p78]-- 2.8.2 Pre-process replenishment
This replenishment starts when the lot processing time in the previous module has expired and the open cover signal for the current bath has turned ON. The replenishment pump starts to replenish the chemical, pumping the number of strokes specified in the “Pre-Repl” user parameter.

===== [p168-168] DANGER > 8.3 Greasing processure (2块) =====
--[p168]-- Use a spatula to apply grease to places where your finger cannot enter.
(4) Move the part along its path several times by hand to spread the grease.
(5) Turn ON the power to the equipment, and in the manual mode, move each moving part a full stroke. Visually check if the grease has been applied to the whole area.
(6) Visually check if there is any extra grease. If there is, first turn OFF the equipment power, then remove the extra grease using a spatula.
--[p168]-- 8.3 Greasing processure
(1) Confirm that the equipment power supply is OFF.
(2) Check if any foreign matter has become stuck to the linear guides or ball screws. If so, wipe it off. Remove the old (discolored) grease with a dust-free cloth as well.
(3) Put clean gloves on your hands, and apply grease to the ball screws and linear guides, especially in the grooves. Spread the grease evenly on the screws and guides with your hands.

===== [p33-33] WARNING > Transfer module functions and safety cautions (5块) =====
--[p33]-- • Each transfer module is located inside the hard frame with a screw-secured cover and automatically performs all the transfer operations necessary for wafer processing. Operators are not allowed to access the transfer modules except the cassette loading and unloading sections.
• The cassette loading and unloading sections are each provided with an area sensor to stop the transfer system whenever any one approaches either section.
--[p33]-- • Whenever an error beyond your control occurs, do not hesitate to contact our office listed at the end of this document.
For environmental protection
--[p33]-- Transfer module functions and safety cautions
• All the transfer modules used in the equipment are conformed to ANSI RIA R15.6 standards.
• The transfer modules transfer the wafers from/into cassettes; or transfer the wafer cassettes among the loading section, the processing module, and the unloading section according to the specified processing programs to perform fully automatic processing.
--[p33]-- • When an error occurs in the equipment, the alarm is activated and the corresponding message appears on the alarm panel. In such a case, contact a maintenance engineer.
• It is dangerous to access to the interior of the module. If an error requires a repair work for the interior of the module, for instance, such work should be performed only by maintenance engineers who have complete knowledge of the equipment.
--[p33]-- • Before approaching the inside of the frame, be sure to turn the equipment power key switch OFF and lock it.
• Before using a remote control, be sure to check that it can properly activate an emergency stop.
• Near the equipment, do not use any device that generates radio frequency energy, such as a transceiver or a portable phone. Using such device causes the equipment malfunction.
• Do not approach the equipment if you are using a heart pacemaker.

===== [p31-31] WARNING > 2.10.1 Chemical mixture (6块) =====
--[p31 [表]]-- • After chemical drainage, be sure to run an amount of water which exceeds the amount of chemical through the drain line (this is not relevant to the equipment on which DI water is not supposed to be used).
Potential risks in the case of a chemical mixture with the following combination
HF | ${ \sf H } _ { 2 } { \sf S } { \sf O } _ { 4 }$ | ${ \sf H } _ { 2 } { \sf O } _ { 2 }$ | $\mathsf { N H } _ { 4 } \mathsf { O H }$
--[p31]-- To avoid serious accidents resulting from the improper handling of chemicals, be sure to fully understand the properties of each chemical to be used as well as the potential risks associated with the mixing of these chemicals.
--[p31]-- $\mathsf { N H } _ { 4 } \mathsf { O H }$ | Suddenexothermicreaction,bumping, and/orwhitish smoke | Suddenexothermicreaction,bumping, and/orwhitish smoke | Suddenexothermicreaction and/orbumping | Vigorousreaction due tothe presence ofmetal
There are various types of potential risks depending on the combination of chemicals to be used.
--[p31]-- ${ \sf H } _ { 2 } { \sf S } { \sf O } _ { 4 }$ | Temperatureincrease (a fewtens of dègrees) | Temperatureincrease (a fewtens of degrees)and/or colorchange to yellow | Temperatureincrease (a fewtens of dègrees) | Suddenexothermicreaction,bumping, and/orwhitish smoke
${ \sf H } _ { 2 } { \sf O } _ { 2 }$ | Temperatureincrease (a fewdegrees) | Occurrence ofpoisonous gasand/or bumping | Temperatureincrease (a fewtens of degrees) | Vigorousreaction due tothe presence ofmetal
--[p31]-- HF | No particularchanges | Temperatureincrease (a fewtens of degrees) | Temperatureincrease (a fewdegrees) | Suddenexothermicreaction,bumping, and/orwhitish smoke
HCL | No particularchanges | Temperatureincrease (a fewtens of degrees)and/or colorchange to yellow | Occurrence ofpoisonous gasand/or bumping | Suddenexothermicreaction,bumping, and/orwhitish smoke
--[p31]-- 2.10.1 Chemical mixture
When supplying, replenishing, or draining chemicals, be sure not to mix different types of chemicals.
• A mixture of different types of chemicals may cause production of a base, abnormal temperature increase, and/or generation of gas.
• If different types of chemicals are to be used, be sure not to supply, replenish, or drain those chemicals concurrently.

--[p10 [表]]-- 1.8.1 Signal words and alert levels
Signal words used in this manual and on this equipment are classified into three types, DANGER, WARNING and CAUTION, depending upon the degree of potential danger. The meanings for each signal word are as follows.
Alert Level | Description
DANGER | Indicates an imminently hazardous situation, which, if not avoided,could result in death or serious injury.

===== [p26-26] WARNING > 2.7 Countermeasures against leakage (1块) =====
--[p26]-- 2.7 Countermeasures against leakage
The equipment is equipped with sensors to detect leakage from the equipment. When the sensor detects a leakage, an alarm is issued to alert the operator.   
If a leakage occurs, stop the equipment and find the location of the leakage. Always attend to leaks immediately.

(b) The heater turns off when the drainage instruction signal turns ON.
(c) Circulation is performed for the time specified in the “Pump delay time” maintenance parameter to cool down the heater.
--[p64]-- (g) When the pipe drainage (fixed at 60 seconds) is completed, the drainage valves (DR1, 2, 3) close, and the switching valve at the drainage side (CH4) closes 1 second later.

===== [p30-30] WARNING > 2.9.1 Air and ${ \bf N } _ { 2 }$ (2块) =====
--[p30]-- 2.9.1 Air and ${ \bf N } _ { 2 }$
$\Nu _ { 2 }$ supply must not be stopped during equipment operation or while the equipment contains chemicals.
• Air supply must not be stopped during equipment operation or while the utilities are connected to the equipment.
$\Nu _ { 2 }$ gas is necessary for pressure sensors and air sensors which detect fluid levels in each bath to operate.
• When stopping the air or $\Nu _ { 2 }$ gas, be sure to clean the processing baths and chemical tanks, and empty them.
--[p30]-- • Before turning OFF the power supply, be sure to drain and clean the processing baths and chemical tanks.
• If the air is stopped, the pneumatic drive mechanisms (such as pneumatic cylinders and valves) will not function correctly.
• Once $\Nu _ { 2 }$ gas is stopped, the pressure sensors for fluid level detection do not work.

===== [p48-48] WARNING > 2.2.1 Chemical level control (5块) =====
--[p48]-- 2.2.1 Chemical level control
The chemical level in the processing bath is controlled by a pressure sensor. The sensor periodically bubbles $\Nu _ { 2 }$ from a tube in the processing bath and monitors the pressure to control the chemical level (amount). The sensor is located on the rear panel of the bath and can be set to detect a maximum of six discrete levels.
Chemical levels in each inner and outer bath are defined below.
--[p48 [表]]-- The levels used and the definitions, however, differ according to the specifications of the equipment and the combination of chemical baths. For the chemical levels detected by the sensors in the equipment and each chemical bath, refer to the Inspection Report for the equipment.
(1) Inner bath sensor 1
Level No. | Level | Description
6 and 5 | Not used
--[p48]-- 2 | Chemical supplylimit level | In a bath that has the potential risk of chemical mixture,chemical supply cannot be performed if the residualchemical or DI water does not reach this level in order toprevent an occurrence of smoking due to an incorrectsupply of chemicals.
--[p48]-- 1 | Empty level | This level confirms the completion of solution drainage.Drainage is only considered completed after this levelsensor turns OFF (i.e., the solution level falls below thislevel) and the 60-second delay timer expires.
--[p48]-- 4 | Lower limit level | This is the lower limit level of chemical for processing.When the sensor detects that the chemical has fallenbelow this level, it generates a “Bath chemical level lowererror.&quot;Note that an error is not issued during chemicalexchange, drainage, bath cleaning, or the transport of a lotby the transfer unit. In chemical bath modules withultrasonic generators, ultrasonic processing is notperformed when this level is detected.
3 | Not used

Preparation before processing

===== [p47-47] WARNING > 2.2 Processing bath (1块) =====
--[p47]-- 2.2 Processing bath
A typical processing bath consists of an inner and outer bath; a protective bath outside the outer bath (in high-temperature processing baths); and an auto-cover to prevent leakage of chemical mist. Chemical circulation proceeds as follows: chemical is fed by pump pressure from the outer bath to the inner bath, which then overflows returning chemical to the outer bath.

--[p179]-- (7) After removing the mark tube from the CMP (for CA) connector, mentioned in step 2, attach this mark tube to the new thermocouple lead wire. (Attach the mark tube to the correct cable.)

(9) Loosen the screw for the relevant lead wire, remove the cut lead wire, and then attach the lead wire for the new thermocouple.
--[p180]-- Thermocouple and resistance temperature sensor replacement procedure (for processing bath) <PT temperature detector for processing bath>

(2) Move the mark tube toward the terminal block in order to prevent the mark tube from falling off the lead wire that is attached to the terminal block.
--[p179]-- (1) Open the cover of the unit $( \Nu _ { 2 }$ panel cover) that contains the connector to which the thermocouple lead wire has been connected.
(2) Move the mark tube toward the CMP (for CA) connector in order to prevent the mark tube from falling off the lead wire that is attached to the connector. ===== [p182-184] 6 Thermocouple replacement procedure (at exit of inline heater) > Preparation (2块) =====
--[p184]-- Preparation
(1) Confirm that no lots exist in the equipment and switch the equipment mode to manual.
(2) Set the “Filter Cleaning” parameter to “300 sec” and the “Bath Cleaning Repeat Count” to “3”.
--[p182]-- Preparation
(1) Set the equipment mode to manual.
(2) Turn the temperature control switch OFF.
(3) Perform bath cleaning at least three times, using the overhead operation panel. (Chemical control is disabled.)
(4) Confirm that there is no chemical remaining in the heater piping section.
(5) If chemical still remains, perform drainage again.
(6) Turn the equipment power OFF and then lock out the equipment in accordance with the lockout procedure.

===== [p42-42] WARNING > Precautions for avoiding electrical shock (1块) =====
--[p42]-- Precautions for avoiding electrical shock
1. Do not remove the covers for the current-carrying sections.
2. Be sure to connect appropriate protective grounding.
3. Before beginning maintenance, be sure to turn OFF the main power source breakers, and lock them and attach tags.
4. If your hands or legs are wet, dry them with a cloth. Always use only dry hands when turning a power switch ON or OFF.
5.
Before turning a power switch ON, give a clear verbal warning to everyone who is in and around the equipment.

2) When the check is completed, stop the operation.

===== [p46-46] WARNING > 1 Interface (1块) =====
--[p46]-- 1 Interface
Interface modules are units for transferring wafers between the Wet Station and the external environment (operator, AGV, etc.). As an example, this section describes interface modules for $^ { 6 6 } \mathrm { C } ^ { 9 }$ series machines, which process wafers loaded in cassettes.
These modules are located at the ends of the equipment. At the wafer delivery side, they are called “Loaders”, and at the wafer discharge side they are called “Unloaders”.

===== [p210-210] 12 Automatic cover opening/closing speed adjustment procedure > 2.2.1 Countermeasures for piping leakage (1块) =====
--[p210]-- 2.2.1 Countermeasures for piping leakage
If leakage occurs from the piping, follow the procedures below.
1) When a leakage sensor alarm has occurred or you have found a leak during a visual inspection, close the manual valve of the chemical source.
2) Confirm that the equipment is in the Cycle Pause mode. If the Cycle Pause switch lamp is turned OFF, press the Cycle Pause switch and wait until the mode changes to Cycle Pause.

===== [p194-194] 10 Pump drive solenoid valve replacement procedure > Restoring/operation check procedure (1块) =====
--[p194]-- Restoring/operation check procedure
(1) Close the $\Nu _ { 2 }$ panel.
(2) Open the circulation pump regulator valve, and adjust the pressure to the specified level.
(3) Confirm that there is no air leakage at the solenoid valve section.
(4) Release the equipment lock and then start up the equipment.
(5) Perform pump circulation, and confirm that there is no air leakage at the solenoid valve.
(6) Switch the equipment mode to automatic.

===== [p66-67] WARNING > 2.5.4 Pump drainage system (3块) =====
--[p66]-- 2.5.4 Pump drainage system
The flowchart and piping diagrams for the pump drainage system (Pump type) are provided below.
Applied bath: SC
Chemical bath modules
(a) The heater turns off when the drainage instruction signal turns ON.
(b) Circulation is performed for the time specified in “Pump delay time” of the maintenance parameter to cool down the heater.
--[p67]-- (c) When the delay time expires, the line switching valve (CH4) opens, and the switching valve at the circulation side (CH3) closes 1 second later.
(d) When the drainage from the outer bath is completed (60 seconds after the empty confirmation timer starts), the bath switching valves (CH1, 2) are switched to the inner bath side.
--[p67]-- (e) When the drainage from the inner bath is completed (60 seconds after the empty confirmation timer starts), the pump stops and the pipe drainage valves (DR1, 2) open At the same time, the bath switching valves are switched back to the outer bath side, and the switching valve at the circulation side opens 1 second later.
(f) When the piping drainage (fixed at 60 seconds) is completed, the drainage valves (DR1, 2) close, and the switching valve at the drainage side (CH4) closes 1 second later.

===== [p41-41] WARNING > Required knowledge for handling chemicals (3块) =====
--[p41]-- In particular, if your skin is directly exposed to hydrofluoric acid, immediately wash the affected area with running water for at least 15 minutes, and then apply an appropriate ointment for hydrofluoric acid burns to the affected area, and be sure to go to hospital to undergo the required medical treatment.
--[p41]-- Required knowledge for handling chemicals
1. Knowledge of chemicals
Read the MSDS to understand the danger of each chemical to be handled. Note that the characteristics of chemicals can be transformed by mixing or heat.
2. Emergency measures
Before handling chemicals, check the MSDS so that the appropriate emergency measures can be immediately taken in the case of an accident.
--[p41]-- If your skin is directly exposed to a chemical, immediately wash the affected area with running water for at least 15 minutes, and then go to hospital to undergo the required medical treatment.

===== [p249-249] 12 Automatic cover opening/closing speed adjustment procedure > 2.1 Deleting a lot (1块) =====
--[p249]-- 2.1 Deleting a lot
The equipment is always monitoring the processing state and current position of lots currently in the equipment. If an already loaded lot needs to be removed for any reason, remove the lot in the manual mode during a Step Pause and delete the lot’s data. When the operation is resumed, the equipment performs a virtual transfer for the removed lot and activates an alarm when the virtual lot reaches the unloader.
When removing an in-process lot, perform the lot deletion according to the following procedure.

(3) Move the mark tube toward the CMP (for CA) connector in order to prevent the mark tube from falling off the lead wire that is attached to the connector. Cut the lead wire at the point approximately 5 cm from the end (the mark tube must be left on the wire), then bend the cable. Be careful not to drop the mark tube while bending.
--[p183]-- There are screws for securing each lead wire.

===== [p76-76] WARNING > 2.7 Bath cleaning (3块) =====
--[p76]-- The SC and CARO baths use Level 4, and the HF bath uses Level 5.
• Depending on equipment specifications, some chemical bath modules do not have a bath cleaning function.
--[p76]-- 2.7 Bath cleaning
Bath cleaning is performed before chemical exchange. First, the expired chemical is drained from the processing bath. Next, DI water is supplied until the bath cleaning level sensor in the outer bath turns ON (at either Level 4 or 5 depending on the chemical used). Then, the DI water is drained and the bath is again rinsed with DI water. Whether or when to perform bath cleaning must be set in the “Bath cleaning” maintenance parameter.
--[p76]-- When the number of chemical exchange operations reaches the number specified in this parameter, a bath cleaning will be performed during the chemical drainage phase of the next chemical exchange sequence. When “0” is set in this parameter, no bath cleaning operations will be performed.
NOTE
• For the bath cleaning level, Level 4 (lower than the DI water determination level) is used for chemicals of high specific gravity, while Level 5 is used for those of low specific gravity.

===== [p121-121] WARNING > 4.1.9 Checking for remaining lots (1块) =====
--[p121]-- 4.1.9 Checking for remaining lots
Visually check and make sure that no lots or foreign objects such as tools have been left in the processing bath line or interface modules. If you find anything, report it to a system manager or supervisor.

===== [p78-78] WARNING > 2.8 Designated amount replenishment (3块) =====
--[p78]-- SC-2 bath module | ${ \mathsf { H C L } } / { \mathsf { H } } _ { 2 } { \mathsf { O } } _ { 2 }$
$\mathsf { H } _ { 3 } \mathsf { P O } _ { 4 }$ bath module | DI water
--[p78 [表]]-- The number of pump strokes per replenishment and/or replenishment interval should be set in the user parameters.
2.8.1 Chemicals replenished with the designated amount
Module | Chemical
CARO bath module | ${ \sf H } _ { 2 } { \sf S } { \sf O } _ { 4 } / { \sf H } _ { 2 } { \sf O } _ { 2 }$
SC-1 bath module | ${ \mathsf { N H } } _ { 4 } { \mathsf { O H } } / { \mathsf { H } } _ { 2 } { \mathsf { O } } _ { 2 }$
--[p78]-- 2.8 Designated amount replenishment
The designated amount replenishment function refills a designated amount of chemical from the measuring tank to a processing bath with the replenishment pump when “Chemical control” is ON (in both automatic and manual modes).
There are both “pre-process replenishment” and “interval replenishment” modes available for designated amount replenishment. Either or both timings can be applied.

===== [p56-57] WARNING > (2) Temperature control (6块) =====
--[p56]-- $( ^ { \circ } \mathrm { C } ) ^ { \dag }$ user parameter. After the chemical temperature exceeds the specified temperature for the thermocontroller, the heater ON ratio changes to the percentage set in the “Heater on ratio (%)” user parameter.
The heater ON ratio control turns ON and OFF the inline heater in cycles of 20 seconds.
(Ex.) When the Heater ON ratio (%) is set to 80%, the inline heater status changes repeatedly as follows:
--[p57]-- • Since a ${ \mathrm { H } } _ { 3 } { \mathrm { P O } } _ { 4 }$ replenishment when the inline heater ON ratio is not 100% lowers the processing bath temperature, the inline heater ON ratio is set to 100% for the time set in the “Heater on repl. (sec.)” user parameter. If the processing bath temperature is still lower than the specified value when the time set in this parameter has elapsed, the inline heater ON ratio remains 100% until it reaches the specified value.
Then the heater ON ratio switches to the specified percentage.
Chemical bath modules
--[p57]-- • Since lot supply while the inline heater ON ratio is not 100% lowers the processing bath temperature, the inline heater ON ratio is set to 100% for the time set in the “Heater on lot-in (sec.)” user parameter. If the processing bath temperature is still lower than the specified value when the time set in this parameter has elapsed, the inline heater ON ratio remains 100% until it reaches the specified value. Then the heater ON ratio switches to the specified percentage.
--[p56]-- it remains ON for 16 seconds and then remains OFF for 4 seconds (during the cycle of 20 seconds).
Chemical bath modules
• Lot supply is prohibited until the chemical temperature has stabilized after the chemical exchange in the ${ \mathrm { H } } _ { 3 } { \mathrm { P O } } _ { 4 }$ bath and the time set in the “Temp. check time (sec.)” maintenance parameter has elapsed.
--[p56]-- (2) Temperature control
The temperature of the ${ \mathrm { H } } _ { 3 } { \mathrm { P O } } _ { 4 }$ bath is controlled by the inline heater attached in the circulation section and the immersion type quartz heater installed in the processing bath. With regard to DIW replenishment, when the TC/OH switch is ON, it depends on the control output by the thermocontroller. When the TC/OH switch is OFF, DIW replenishment can be carried out by manual operation if the chemical control is inactive.
--[p56]-- The temperature control by each heater is managed independently, as described below.
(1) Immersion heater
After the chemical supply has been completed during the chemical exchange sequence, turning ON the TC/OH switch for the processing bath sets the heater ON ratio to 100%.
(2) Inline heater
After the chemical supply has been completed during the chemical exchange sequence, the heater ON ratio is set to 100% when the chemical temperature reaches the value set in the “Pump start temp.

===== [p207-207] 12 Automatic cover opening/closing speed adjustment procedure > 1.2.1 Restart of the equipment (2块) =====
--[p207]-- 1.2.1 Restart of the equipment
1) Turning ON the equipment power
To turn ON the equipment power, turn ON the MAIN POWER key switch on the main panel. The equipment then starts up in the manual mode. Also, turn ON the power to both the cleaning unit and the transfer unit. The PC of the equipment automatically starts up and shows the GUI operation display on the screen.
2) Perform the log-in operation.
NOTE
--[p207]-- There is no need to perform the log-in operation if the equipment power recovers before the shutdown procedure is automatically performed on the GUI.
3) Reset the alarms on the equipment.
“Power failure detection” and “Ethernet communication error” are the regular alarms that occur when there is an equipment power failure.

===== [p125-125] CAUTION > 5.1 Switching from manual mode to automatic mode (2块) =====
--[p125]-- 5.1 Switching from manual mode to automatic mode
Modes can be switched by pressing the AUTO key on the main operation panel when the following conditions are met. When the lamp of “AUTO” is lit, it indicates that the equipment is in automatic mode.
NOTE
If the lamp remains off even after the “AUTO” key is pressed, one possible cause is that a moving section is still not located at its home position. Retry home return and check if the alarm is still activated.
--[p125]-- Possible in one of following states when cover interlocks are in the “NORMAL” state.
• all moving sections are at their respective home positions (the lamp of the HOME key is ON) and there are no lots in the equipment; or
• the equipment has stopped and is in the Step Pause state.

===== [p46-46] WARNING > 1.1.1 Loader (1块) =====
--[p46]-- 1.1.1 Loader
This type of loader receives wafers which have already been loaded in a processing cassette by an external wafer setting unit. ===== [p19-19] WS-820/MTR series manuals > 1.21.2 Transfer unit (1块) =====
--[p19]-- 1.21.2 Transfer unit
This equipment is provided with transfer units in order to transfer wafers (substrates) for processing. If precautions are not taken carefully, there could be serious danger to the operators. To prevent such hazardous situations, the equipment is provided with a variety of interlocking safety devices for the transfer units. Never operate the equipment while the safety devices are disabled as this is extremely dangerous.

===== [p54-54] WARNING > 2.4 Circulation system (1块) =====
--[p54]-- 2.4 Circulation system
The chemical solution in a processing bath is circulated between the outer bath and the inner bath by the circulation pump. A heater or an electric thermocontroller is provided on the circulation path to control the temperature of the chemical. The chemical is also cleaned with a filter. For the specifications of the circulation system for each chemical bath, refer to the “Mutually Agreed and Confirmed Specifications” of the equipment.

===== [p26-26] WARNING > 2.6.1 Exhaust (1块) =====
--[p26]-- 2.6.1 Exhaust
Never turn OFF the exhaust system.
If it is turned off or the exhaust pressure is reduced, the chemical gases in the equipment may leak and adversely affect personnel. A similar situation may occur if the butterfly valve or slit in the exhaust section is closed or the exhaust scrubber on the primary side (on the customer’s facility side) is stopped.

===== [p86-86] WARNING > 3.2.3 EDR bath module (2块) =====
--[p86 [表]]-- 3.2.3 EDR bath module
For rinsing in the EDR bath module, the processing time T (duration from wafer delivery request to wafer discharge request) should be specified in the recipe setup window. Then rinsing is performed according to the times set for T5 and T6 (each specified in the user parameters) within the total time T.
Parameter | Description
T5: Drain time | Upflow stops and quick drainage is performed after the wafer discharge iscompleted. At the same time showering starts.
--[p86]-- T6: Upflow time | The quick drainage valve closes, upflow starts and showering continues.

===== [p18-18] WS-820/MTR series manuals > 1.19 Maintenance (1块) =====
--[p18]-- 1.19 Maintenance
When performing maintenance work, strictly comply with the following precautions.

===== [p112-112] WARNING > 2.2 To shut down the equipment for a short period: (1块) =====
--[p112]-- 2.2 To shut down the equipment for a short period:
1) Select the [Parameter] and then the [Maintenance Parameter] menus. Set “Chemical control” for all the chemical baths to “0” (OFF) and drain each processing bath.
2) Close the supply valves (toggle valves) to the measuring tanks, and empty them by transferring their contents to the processing baths.
3) Execute bath cleaning for all the chemical processing baths.
4) Fill up both the inner and outer baths with DI water to prevent their filters from drying.

===== [p42-42] WARNING > Precautions for safety in high places (2块) =====
--[p42]-- Be careful to protect the equipment and parts from suffering damage that may potentially occur during maintenance work in unsteady places.
NOTE
• Confirm that there are no potentially hazardous conditions that may result in a collision with transferred objects.
• While performing maintenance, pay sufficient attention to protect the ULPA and HEPA from suffering damage.
--[p42]-- Precautions for safety in high places
1. When using a stepladder, confirm that its open angle is securely locked.
2. When using a stepladder, avoid standing on the top or second to top step.
3. When using a stool, confirm that it is firmly positioned.
4. Only one engineer may stand on a stool.
5. Confirm that the working conditions have been properly arranged so that engineers will not drop tools or parts easily.
6.

===== [p163-165] 7.1 Operation check for the maintenance cover sensors Equipment mode > Restart procedure (2块) =====

(1) Close each maintenance cover and lock it with the corresponding setscrew.
(2) Turn the power supply for the transfer units ON and reset the alarms.
(3) Switch the equipment mode from manual to automatic.
--[p165]-- Restart procedure
(1) Confirm that there are no tools or instruments left in the equipment.
(2) Confirm that no leakage errors remain.
(3) Confirm that all the removed maintenance covers are reattached.
(4) Open the chemical demand manual valves.
(5) Turn ON all the temperature control switches.
(6) Switch the equipment mode from manual to automatic.

===== [p19-19] WS-820/MTR series manuals > 1.21.1 Transfer module functions and safety cautions (2块) =====
--[p19]-- 1.21.1 Transfer module functions and safety cautions
Each transfer module is located inside covers that are secured with screws or other parts and automatically performs all the transfer operations necessary for wafer processing. This ensures operator safety as the transfer sections are accessible only when setting and resetting the cassettes.
When an area sensor is installed in a cassette setting or resetting section, transfer in this section will also be halted if you access it carelessly.
--[p19]-- Accessing internal transfer modules is extremely dangerous. Therefore, when it is necessary to access the transfer section, such as for maintenance work, be sure to assign only the maintenance service engineers who have complete knowledge of the equipment, to perform the work.

===== [p41-41] WARNING > 1.4.3 Dangers of transfer mechanisms (1块) =====
--[p41]-- 1.4.3 Dangers of transfer mechanisms
This equipment is provided with several transfer robots and mechanisms that are used to move wafers among various processing units. If you fail to exercise sufficient caution, you could suffer serious dangers, such as being caught or pinched by moving sections. As preventive measures against these hazards, a variety of safety interlocks are provided for the transfer robots and mechanisms.
1. Do not perform maintenance while any interlock is canceled.
2.
Attach the specified safety tools.
3. Remove any string or accessories that could be caught by moving sections.

===== [p174-174] 3 Replacement of circulation filter (cartridge type) > Operation check procedure (3块) =====
--[p174]-- 3) When the circulation pump is working (i.e., the filter is being cleaned) during the bath cleaning, measure the circulation pressure with the pressure gauge. If the circulation pressure is remarkably higher than usual, the filter is not hydrophilic enough. Soak the filter with IPA (IPA replacement), and then restart the operation check from step 1.
--[p174]-- Operation check procedure
1) Perform bath cleaning using the overhead operation panel. Keep the parameter setting values changed during steps 1 through 3 of the “Work procedure”.
2) Without removing the cover, visually confirm during the bath cleaning that no leakage occurs around the filter unit. If you find any leakage, stop the bath cleaning and further tighten the part causing the leakage. Then restart the operation check from step 1.
--[p174]-- 4) After the bath cleaning is completed, remove the maintenance cover for the piping section and confirm that there is no leakage around the filter unit.
5) Re-attach the maintenance cover for the piping section and clear all the currently issued alarms.
6) Restore the parameters you changed during step 1 through 3 with the previously recorded values.
7) Perform chemical exchange using the overhead operation panel.

===== [p163-166] 7.1 Operation check for the maintenance cover sensors Equipment mode > Inspection procedure (5块) =====
--[p164]-- Inspection procedure
(1) Circulation and measuring
1) Visually confirm that there is no leakage in the filter vat, lower vat, or measuring vat, or from the fittings in each module.
2) In the rear maintenance area, remove the maintenance covers from the circulation and measuring sections. (An error will occur and circulation in the chemical baths will stop.)
3) Put a clean cloth moistened with water on the sensing section of the leakage detectors on the filter vat, lower vat, and measuring vat.
--[p163]-- Inspection procedure
(1) Sliding cover sensors
1) Open one of the sliding covers and check that the corresponding “door open” alarm is activated.
2) Close the cover and reset the alarm.
3) In the same way, open each of the interlocked maintenance covers (the front covers of the main body, the peripheral covers of the LD/UL, the circulation section covers and the measuring section covers) and confirm that the corresponding “door open” alarm is activated.
--[p166]-- Inspection procedure
--[p164]-- For the organic type equipment’s leakage detection, therefore, follow the steps below.
1) Turn ON the OL switch.
2) Confirm that the corresponding leakage alarm occurs.
--[p164]-- Confirm that the corresponding leakage alarm occurs.
4) Remove the cloth from the detector and wipe the sensing section with a dry clean cloth.
5) Reset the “\*\* leakage” error.
6) Reattach the maintenance cover.
Note 1: Inspection procedure for the organic type equipment’s leakage detection
Since an OL switch is used in the organic type equipment, the inspection steps for the organic type equipment are different from those for the ordinary equipment.

===== [p183-186] 6 Thermocouple replacement procedure (at exit of inline heater) > Restoring/operation check procedure (2块) =====
--[p183]-- Restoring/operation check procedure
(1) Confirm that there are no tools remaining in the equipment.
(2) Confirm that all the detached covers are attached.
(3) Release the equipment lock and then start up the equipment.
(4) Perform bath cleaning using the overhead operation panel. Confirm that there is no leakage during the operation.
(5) Turn the temperature control switch ON. Perform chemical exchange and confirm that there is no leakage.
If there is, immediately stop the operation and take appropriate measures.
(6) Switch the equipment mode to automatic.
--[p186]-- Restoring/operation check procedure
(1) Confirm that there are no tools remaining in the equipment.
(2) Re-attach all the detached maintenance covers.
(3) Release the equipment lock and then start up the equipment.
(4) Perform bath cleaning and confirm that there is no leakage at the fittings.
(5) Turn the temperature control switch ON and perform chemical exchange using the overhead operation panel.
(6) Confirm that the temperature control has been completed.
(7) Confirm that there is no leakage.
(8) Return the changed parameters to their original values.
(9) Switch the equipment mode to automatic.

===== [p210-210] 12 Automatic cover opening/closing speed adjustment procedure > WARNING (1块) =====
--[p210]-- WARNING
When performing the system restoration procedures after canceling a leakage alarm, be sure to implement local exhaust for the module from which there is a leakage, and wear protective gear, including a protective mask, protective glasses, and a gas mask.
1) Stop chemical supply to the relevant module and eliminate the cause of the leakage.
2) Detach the leakage sensor from the bracket.
3) Sufficiently wipe off the area from which the leakage occurred.
4) Reattach the sensor to its original position.
5) Reset the alarm.

===== [p27-27] WARNING > 2.8 Safety locks and tags (1块) =====
--[p27]-- 2.8 Safety locks and tags
Locking brackets are provided for the power switch, chemical valves, and covers of this equipment in order to prevent any accident that may be caused by inadvertent supply of electricity/fluids during maintenance work.
The equipment must not be operated when the brackets are locked or when the equipment has tags affixed that show precautions concerning the locked areas in order to prevent anyone from touching the equipment.

===== [p84-84] WARNING > 3.2.1 H/C-QDR bath module (2块) =====
--[p84]-- T1: Delay time | Upflow is switched from hot DI water to plain DI water, bubblingstarts and continues.
T2: Drain time | Upflow and bubbling stop, quick drainage is performed, andshowering starts at the same time. (Showering is not performed duringquick drainage before wafer delivery.)
T3: Upflow time | The quick drainage valve closes, upflow starts and showeringcontinues.
T4: Standby time | Bubbling starts, showering stops and upflow continues.
Rinsing bath module
--[p84 [表]]-- 3.2.1 H/C-QDR bath module
For rinsing in the H/C-QDR bath module, the processing time T (duration from wafer delivery request to wafer discharge request) should be specified in the recipe setup window. Then rinsing is performed according to the times set for T0 \~ T4 (each specified in user parameters) within the total time T.
Parameter | Description
T0: Hot DI water upflowtime | After the wafers are supplied, upflow of hot DI water and bubblingstart during this time.

===== [p111-111] WARNING > 2.1 Shut-down procedure (3块) =====
--[p111]-- 2.1 Shut-down procedure
When shutting down the equipment, observe the procedure and the notes described below. Please also note that the method for shutting down the equipment (including its utilities) differs depending on the intended period of shut-down.
1) Use the Lot Tracking window to confirm that no lots remain in the equipment. Select the [Operation] and then the [Lot Tracking] menus. The following display appears.
--[p111]-- If any remaining lots are found in the equipment, remove them by completing their entire processing sequence.
2) Press the “AUTO” switch to change the mode to manual. The switch’s internal lamp turns OFF when the mode is changed to manual.
To change the mode to manual, the equipment must be in either Robot pause, Step pause, or Cycle pause. Be sure to set the equipment to an inactive state beforehand.
3) Visually check once again that no lots remain in the equipment.
--[p111]-- For baths with auto-covers, open them manually for checking.
4) If any lot remains, remove it manually and delete the data in the Lot Tracking dialog.
5) Select the relevant shut-down procedure from the following list depending upon the equipment’s expected period of inactivity.
System shutdown

===== [p51-51] WARNING > 2.3 Measuring tank (1块) =====
--[p51]-- 2.3 Measuring tank
Each chemical used in chemical bath modules is pre-measured and supplied according to the mixing ratio, and mixed in the processing bath during chemical exchange. Some measuring tanks have a pre-heating function to heat up measured chemicals to a specified temperature. Each chemical is supplied to its respective measuring tank from chemical utility lines through piping.
When a certain chemical must be supplied to several different measuring tanks, it is distributed from the piping with an array of valves.

===== [p51-51] WARNING > 2.3.1 Chemical level control (1块) =====
--[p51]-- 2.3.1 Chemical level control

===== [p41-41] WARNING > 1.4.2 Dangers of high temperature areas (1块) =====
--[p41]-- 1.4.2 Dangers of high temperature areas
Some chemical baths are used at high temperature in this equipment.
Never touch the heater-fitted components and their surrounding piping lines.
1. Avoid approaching the high temperature areas.
2. If a high temperature control is being conducted, confirm sufficient decrease of the temperature after turning OFF the temperature control system and heaters.

===== [p207-207] 12 Automatic cover opening/closing speed adjustment procedure > 1.2.2 Lot processing status after the equipment is restarted (2块) =====
--[p207]-- 1.2.2 Lot processing status after the equipment is restarted
The lot that is being processed in a chemical bath will remain immersed in the bath either until it is instructed by remote control or until home return is carried out. If the lot is being processed in the LPD unit or a rinsing bath, the processing will be interrupted and DI water trickling will then be performed.
If the lot is being dried in the LPD unit, depressurization will be interrupted.
--[p207]-- In this case, and depending on the processing timing, the “LPD IPA vapor exist. Drying require” alarm may occur. If this alarm occurs, press the START key on the overhead operation panel to activate the pressure-reducing pump. (The alarm will automatically be reset when the pressure in the unit is reduced to the specified level.) Note that if depressurization is executed, purge the chamber with $\Nu _ { 2 }$ to restore the inner pressure to the level of the atmospheric pressure.
If the transfer units main halted
If the transfer units are carrying cassettes or wafers, they will remain halted.

===== [p218-218] 12 Automatic cover opening/closing speed adjustment procedure > 2) Countermeasures (2块) =====
--[p218]-- 2) Countermeasures
When the equipment has entered the CYCLE PAUSE state due to an error, do not load a new lot but locate the error and find the cause. Take the appropriate measures outlined in the troubleshooting tables in the next section.
When the equipment has stopped due to an error in the transfer system, identify the error and check the equipment condition first to judge if the remaining lots can be discharged from the equipment.
--[p218]-- If the lot discharge is judged to be possible, reset the alarm (by turning the transfer unit’s power back on and resetting the alarm on the main operation panel) and discharge all lots from the equipment. Then, locate the error, find the cause and correct it. On the other hand, if the lot discharge operation is likely to damage the lot and/or the equipment as determined from the type of error and condition of the equipment, correct the error first and then perform the alarm reset operation.

===== [p18-18] WS-820/MTR series manuals > 1.20.1 Locks (2块) =====
--[p18]-- Authorized employees are persons who are trained and proficient in performing a hazardous energy isolation (lockout/tagout) procedure on an energy source ensuring that the energy isolating device and the equipment being controlled cannot be operated until the locking device is removed.
--[p18]-- 1.20.1 Locks
Before attempting maintenance work, ensure that the power switch and source valves for gases and chemical solutions are locked and that the key is retained by an authorized employee so that only the authorized employee can operate the equipment.
Quoted from SEMI S19-1102 What is an “authorized employee”?

===== [p36-36] WARNING > 1. Required items (3块) =====
--[p36]-- 1. Required items
This equipment contains processing functions that use extremely hazardous chemicals, and transfer units that can move at high speed.
Before beginning maintenance, prepare the following protective gear and other required items, and check them for any defects.
--[p36]-- 1) Check the conditions of the target equipment.
2) Check the maintenance procedures to be performed.
Check the maintenance procedures and precautions.
3) Conduct a danger prediction survey (KY method) to check the potential hazards of the maintenance procedures.
4) Check the estimated man-hours and the time schedule.
5) In case of emergency, check the locations of the emergency shower, hand wash stand, and eye rinsing device, and also prepare appropriate rinse water.
If you have any questions, contact our branch office listed on the back page of this manual.
--[p36]-- Chemical resistant gloves, chemical resistant arm covers, protective goggles, chemical resistant apron, protective mask, ointment for hydrofluoric acid burns, pH paper, clean wipers, protective sheets, containers, plastic bags, tags (for indicating that an adjustment is underway or other cautions), safety tools (transfer unit stoppers, etc.), safety belt, rubber boots, worktable, etc.
2. Preliminary meeting (Confirmation checks before beginning maintenance)

===== [p12-12] WS-820/MTR series manuals > 1.9.1 Switching operation of power supply (1块) =====
--[p12]-- 1.9.1 Switching operation of power supply
The equipment incorporates high-voltage circuit. Touching any part of your body to such circuits can cause serious injury and even death. BE EXTREMELY CAREFUL not to touch these circuits, especially with wet hands.
Various power supply switches are installed in the equipment. Be sure to turn these switches ON or OFF in the designated order. If the proper order is not followed, unexpected movement of certain parts could cause serious injury.
Therefore, allow only authorized personnel to operate these switches.

===== [p81-81] WARNING > 2.10.2 Air vent operation (2块) =====
--[p81]-- 2.10.2 Air vent operation
An air vent operation is performed on filters at specified intervals while the circulation pump is running to release air trapped in the filters.
First, when the circulation pump starts operation, it circulates its solution through the air venting line continuously for 15 seconds. After that, the circulation path is switched to a filtered line for the period specified in the “Filter air vent interval” maintenance parameter.
--[p81]-- When the filtered circulation time reaches the value set in “Filter air vent interval”, the circulation line is again switched to the air vent line for 3 seconds. This cycle is continuously repeated.
Chemical bath modules

===== [p41-41] WARNING > 1.4.4 Dangers of handling electricity (1块) =====
--[p41]-- 1.4.4 Dangers of handling electricity
Electrical shock generally occurs due to the passage of electric current through the human body. The severity of electrical shock is widely diverse: It may be just a perception of electric current, or lead to pain or muscle rigidity, or at worst could result in death. Therefore, be sure to take sufficient care even if the voltage is low.
