===== [p189-189] 8 Circulation pump replacement procedure > Restoring/operation check procedure (2块) =====
--[p189]-- Restoring/operation check procedure
(1) Check if all the piping has been completed.
(2) Confirm that there are no tools remaining in the equipment.
(3) Re-attach the maintenance cover.
(4) Open the circulation pump regulator valve, and adjust the drive pressure to the level noted on the nameplate.
(5) Unlock the equipment and then start up the equipment.
(6) Perform bath cleaning and flushing using manual operation. Confirm that there is no chemical/air leakage.
--[p189]-- If there is, immediately stop the operation and tighten up the relevant section.
(7) Turn the temperature control switch ON and perform chemical exchange. Confirm that there is no chemical leakage during the operation.
(8) If there is leakage, immediately stop the operation and take appropriate measures.
(9) Return the changed parameters to their original values.
(10) Switch the equipment mode to automatic.

===== [p55-55] WARNING > (1) Bypassing circulation (2块) =====
--[p55]-- (1) Bypassing circulation
Since ${ \mathrm { H } } _ { 3 } { \mathrm { P O } } _ { 4 }$ is highly viscous at low temperatures and is thus difficult to filter, the chemical is circulated along path 1 (shown in the figure below) by opening the filter's air vent valve when temperature control starts.
Two parameters “Pump start temp.” and “Pump stop temp.” are available in the user parameters for ${ \mathrm { H } } _ { 3 } { \mathrm { P O } } _ { 4 }$ .
--[p55]-- Before the temperature reaches the circulation start temperature, the chemical is circulated bypassing the filter (path 1 in the figure). When the temperature reaches the circulation start temperature, the path is switched to path 2 in the figure and the chemical passes through the filter. When the temperature falls below the circulation stop temperature after the path is switched to path 2, the path is switched back and the chemical is again circulated along path 1.
Chemical bath modules

===== [p195-195] 11 Water saving type pneumatic valve flow rate adjustment procedure > [Water trickling adjustment] (1块) =====
--[p195]-- [Water trickling adjustment]
2) Turn OFF DI water supply and turn the head of the pneumatic valve that contains the water trickling function in the counterclockwise direction. The head section will be removed.
3) Turn OFF DI water supply and unlock the handle of the flow rate for water trickling.
4) When the bypass flow rate has been adjusted with the flow rate handle (for water trickling), lock the handle with the lock pin.
5) Turn OFF DI water supply and turn the head of the pneumatic valve that contains the water trickling function in the clockwise direction.

===== [p26-26] WARNING > Manostar Switch and Lower Limit Setting (for normal operation) (1块) =====
--[p26]-- Manostar Switch and Lower Limit Setting (for normal operation)
The exhaust pressure’s lower limit detected by the manostar switch has been set to 20 mm H<sub>2</sub>O (200 Pa/0.029 psi).
When an exhaust pressure of 20 mm $\mathrm { H } _ { 2 } \mathrm { O }$ (200 Pa/0.029 psi) or less is detected, an alarm will be issued.
Model: MS-61L-VT 20 Pa to 118 Pa (YAMAMOTO ELECTRIC WORKS)
Set pressure: 20 Pa to 118 Pa

===== [p83-83] WARNING > 3.1.2 Solution level control (1块) =====
--[p83]-- 3.1.2 Solution level control
The solution level in the rinsing bath modules is controlled by pressure sensors as in the chemical baths.
Lower limit level sensors (Level 4 = level to ensure sufficient solution to cover wafer topes) and empty level sensors (Level 1 = level to confirm complete drainage from the bath) are used to control levels.

===== [p189-189] 8 Circulation pump replacement procedure > How to handle the replaced parts (1块) =====
--[p189]-- How to handle the replaced parts
The replaced parts should be disposed of as industrial wastes.
(1) Rinse the replaced parts to eliminate all remaining chemical.
(2) Dry the parts.
(3) Securely pack the parts so that any chemical remaining inside will not leak out.
(4) Write down the parts names, materials and chemicals used.
(5) If special ventilation is necessary for safety, request that a qualified specialist handle the parts as industrial wastes.
In cases where storage is necessary, keep them in a designated safe place.
Measuring tank immersion heater replacement procedure

--[p25]-- 2.5.3 Cover interlock
When a safety cover installed in the equipment is opened, an interlock is activated and operation of the equipment is halted.
Cover interlocks are provided for the transfer system and the chemical system.
For details on the cover interlocks, refer to Appendix O1.2.5.3.

===== [p177-177] CAUTION > Disposal of replaced parts (2块) =====

Thermocouple and resistance temperature sensor replacement procedure (for processing bath)
--[p177]-- Disposal of replaced parts
After replacement, clean the old parts and dispose of them as industrial waste.
1) Clean the old parts with water to remove residual chemicals.
2) Wipe off any water from the parts.
3) Pack each part. Take care that nothing can leak from the packed part.
4) Write down the name and material of the part and the name of the chemical adhered to it.
5) When you want to dispose of the part, commission a special contractor to handle it as industrial waste.

===== [p110-110] WARNING > DANGER (2块) =====
--[p110]-- DANGER
• When handling chemical solutions, working on chemical piping, or standing where chemical solutions are exposed to the atmosphere, the possibility of a fatal injury is high if you directly touch chemicals or inhale chemical fumes. When performing such work, always wear appropriate protective gear, such as the specified face guards, goggles, gloves, gas masks, etc.
• When handling IPA, always wear the appropriate protective gear and be careful of the ventilation.
--[p110]-- Also never allow any flame or combustible substances in the vicinity of the work area. Explosions or fires could occur.
• The IPA supply unit contains nitrogen gas. Asphyxiation could occur if the N purge valve is not closed and proper ventilation not ensured before opening the cover or performing maintenance of the unit.

===== [p120-120] WARNING > 4.1.7 Checking utilities (1块) =====
--[p120]-- 4.1.7 Checking utilities
Perform the following checks on the rear panel of the equipment.
1) Confirm that supply pressures for air and $\Nu _ { 2 }$ are normal on the relevant flow meters.
2) Confirm that the exhaust pressure is normal on the exhaust manostar gauge.
3) Confirm that the chemical supply valves on the connections between the chemical sources and the equipment are all open.
4) Confirm that the supply valves for DI water, cooling water and plain water are all open.

===== [p214-214] 12 Automatic cover opening/closing speed adjustment procedure > 3.2.2 Display of alarm description (2块) =====
--[p214]-- 3.2.2 Display of alarm description
When you select the [Alarm] menu, the following window appears, showing the description of the alarm.
(1) Reset All . . . . . . Cancels all alarms displayed in the window. However, an alarm whose cause remains unsolved will not be cancelled.
(2) Reset . . . . . . . . . Only the selected alarm can be cancelled. However, an alarm whose cause is not removed cannot be cancelled.
(3) Status Disp . . . . Displays the status window of the unit for the selected alarm.
--[p214]-- The current status of the unit in which the alarm occurred can be checked in the window.
(4) Details. . . . . . . . Displays the details of the selected alarm.

===== [p72-72] WARNING > 2) Automatic chemical exchange 1 (1块) =====
--[p72]-- 2) Automatic chemical exchange 1
When the chemical life expires in a bath, lots with recipes that specify processing in the expired bath are not supplied from the loader and chemical exchange starts. However, if wafers to be processed in the expired bath are already located in an expired bath or upstream module (except for the loader) at the time of chemical expiration, chemical exchange starts after these wafers are processed and discharged.

===== [p100-100] WARNING > 4.2 IPA vapor dryer (1块) =====
--[p100]-- 4.2 IPA vapor dryer
This module dries wet wafers by replacing the water with IPA (isopropyl alcohol). Wafers are placed in the bath with IPA vapor for a specified time to dry.

===== [p88-88] WARNING > 3.3 Resistivity check (1块) =====
--[p88]-- 3.3 Resistivity check
The FR bath module is located last in the stream of chemical and rinsing bath modules, and performs final rinsing of the processed wafers. During this process, it also checks the adequacy of its rinsing by measuring the resistivity of DI water in its bath.

===== [p193-193] 10 Pump drive solenoid valve replacement procedure > Preparation (3块) =====
--[p193]-- Preparation
(1) Turn the equipment power OFF and then lock out the equipment in accordance with the lockout procedure.
(2) Close the circulation pump regulator valve to set the drive pressure to “0”.
(3) Open the cover of the unit (N<sub>2</sub> panel cover) that contains the solenoid valve to be replaced. (Handle the wiring carefully.)
Replacement procedure
(1) Cut the connector cable of the solenoid valve at the point approximately 5 cm from the end (the mark tube must be left on the cable).
--[p193]-- Then, bend the cable so that the tube will not fall off.
(2) After removal of the screws that are securing the solenoid valve to the base, remove the solenoid valve.
(3) Remove the fitting and the dissipative muffler from the solenoid valve.
(Put a mark on the removed fitting and dissipative muffler for correct piping.)
(4) Wind sealing tape around both the fitting and muffler (removed in step 3) and attach them to a new solenoid valve.
(5) Secure the new solenoid valve to the base.
--[p193]-- (6) Implement terminal treatment for the new solenoid valve cable, and attach this cable to the connector.
Pump drive solenoid valve replacement procedure

===== [p41-41] WARNING > 1.4.1 Dangers of chemicals (1块) =====
--[p41]-- 1.4.1 Dangers of chemicals
The chemicals used in this equipment are extremely dangerous.
Contact with chemical liquid or gas may cause skin irritation, burns or loss of eyesight, and breathing in chemical gas may result in serious injuries and even death. Moreover, an
explosion or fire can occur if the chemicals are flammable.
To ensure safe handling of chemicals, study the characteristics of the chemicals being used in the equipment and strictly follow the safety instructions.

===== [p101-101] WARNING > 4.3 Low pressure drying unit (LPD) (1块) =====
--[p101]-- 4.3 Low pressure drying unit (LPD)
This unit rinses wafers with up-flowing DI water. Then, the unit is drained and filled with IPA vapor to replace the liquid with IPA. Next, the pressure in the unit is reduced and it dries wafers as it is purged with $\Nu _ { 2 }$

===== [p20-20] WS-820/MTR series manuals > 2.1.4 Intended use (1块) =====
--[p20]-- 2.1.4 Intended use
This equipment is a wafer wet rinsing device. It is positioned as one of the semiconductor manufacturing devices and is used during wafer processing. In this equipment, wafers are dipped in the chemical baths in order to rinse (remove) foreign objects such as particles, metal or organic matter from their surfaces.

===== [p192-192] 9 Measuring tank immersion heater replacement procedure > How to handle the replaced parts (1块) =====
--[p192]-- How to handle the replaced parts
The replaced parts should be disposed of as industrial wastes.
(1) Rinse the replaced parts to eliminate all remaining chemical.
(2) Dry the parts.
(3) Securely pack the parts so that any chemical remaining inside will not leak out.
(4) Write down the parts names, materials and chemicals used.

In cases where storage is necessary, keep them in a designated safe place.

===== [p58-58] WARNING > (2) Pump drainage system (1块) =====
--[p58]-- (2) Pump drainage system
Drains using pump pressure. The chemical path is switched from the circulation side to the drainage side while the pump continues to operate. The pump drainage system is further divided into two types. In one type, the drained chemical is stored in the cooling tank until it cools down to a specified temperature and is then drained into the factory drainage line. In the other type, the chemical drains directly into the factory drainage line.

===== [p54-54] WARNING > (1) Conditions to start temperature control (2块) =====
--[p54]-- • “Bath high temperature error”
• “Circulation pump alarm”
• “Circulation pump leak”
• “In-line heater leak”
• ${ } ^ { 6 6 } \mathrm { N } _ { 2 }$ low pressure error”
$^ { 6 6 } \mathrm { A i r }$ low pressure error”
• “Auto-cover open”
--[p54]-- (1) Conditions to start temperature control
Temperature control starts when “TC” switch is turned on only when all the following conditions apply.
(a) The heater protection lower limit level sensors in both the inner and outer processing baths are ON.
(b) Solution is not being supplied, the bath is not being rinsed or solution is not being drained.
(c) The circulation pump is operating.
(d) The following alarms are not being generated.
• “Bath/heater overheat alarm”

===== [p146-146] 5.2 Temperature control check (Chemical baths and LPD) Equipment mode > Be sure to wear protective gear. (1块) =====

• Processing bath baffle plate, gangway and auto cover # LPD auto cover and gangway
1) Open the sliding cover.
2) Remove the baffle plate to clean the unit.
3) Processing bath: Clean the auto cover and gangway with a hand shower.
4) Clean the baffle plate with a clean cloth moistened with DI water in the draft.
5) LPD: Clean the auto cover and gangway with a clean cloth moistened with DI water.
6) Transfer unit chuck: Clean with a hand shower the vertical sides of the chuck at the rinse bath position.

===== [p178-178] 5 Thermocouple and resistance temperature sensor replacement procedure (for processing bath) > CAUTION (1块) =====
--[p178]-- CAUTION
• Before beginning the replacement, turn OFF the power source breaker of the equipment, lock the breaker out, and attach tags to the breaker so that power is not supplied to the equipment. The lock-out key must be kept by the authorized employee after locking the breaker.
• Do not touch any component other than the wiring and sensors required for the replacement.
• When the replacement is completed, close the maintenance cover and lock it with a key.
After locking, the key must be removed from the lock and kept by the manager.
• Wear appropriate protective gear.

===== [p172-172] 3 Replacement of circulation filter (cartridge type) > Preparation (1块) =====
--[p172]-- Preparation
1) IPA replacement for filter
1. After wearing protective gear, put the filter into the measuring cylinder and inject IPA until the filter is completely submerged. Use a draft for this work.
2. Tap the measuring cylinder until the filter does not foam.
3. Leave them as they are for at least 5 hours.
4. Just before attaching the filter, dump the IPA you injected.
2) After confirming that no lot remains in the equipment, set the operation mode to manual.

===== [p82-82] WARNING > 2.11 Ultrasonic processing (SC bath module) (1块) =====
--[p82]-- 2.11 Ultrasonic processing (SC bath module)
The SC bath can be equipped with an ultrasonic processing function to improve the wafer rinsing effect. DI water is filled to a certain level between the processing bath and its external protection bath, and sound waves from the ultrasonic generator at the bottom of the protection bath are indirectly transmitted to the processing bath.

===== [p19-19] WS-820/MTR series manuals > 1.21.3 Other precautions (1块) =====
--[p19]-- 1.21.3 Other precautions
Do not use devices that generate a radio frequency of 3 kHz to 300 GHz, such as wireless telephones or transceivers, in the vicinity of the equipment. This could cause equipment malfunction.
Do not approach the equipment if you are using a heart pacemaker. This could cause malfunction of the heart pacemaker.

===== [p61-61] WARNING > 2.5.2 Aspirator drainage system (Two-aspirator type) (2块) =====
--[p61]-- 2.5.2 Aspirator drainage system (Two-aspirator type)
The flowchart and the piping diagram for the aspirator drainage system (One-aspirator type) are shown below.
Applied bath: SC
(1) Flowchart
(a) The heater turns off when the drainage instruction signal turns ON.
(b) Circulation is performed for the time specified in the “Pump delay time” maintenance parameter to cool down the heater.
--[p61]-- (c) The aspirators (AS1, AS2) turn on and the drainage valves in the inner bath (DR5) and outer bath (DR4) open.
(d) When the empty confirmation time for the inner bath (fixed at 60 seconds) expires, the aspirator (AS2) stops and the pipe drainage valves (DR1, 2, 3) open.
(e) When drainage is completed, the drainage valves (DR1, 2, 3) close and the aspirator (AS1) stops.

===== [p9-9] WS-820/MTR series manuals > 1.1 Thoroughly read the manual first! (2块) =====
--[p9]-- In this manual, section 1 describes the general guidelines and precautions for safe usage of this equipment and section 2 describes the specific information and precautions for the equipment you have purchased. Be sure to thoroughly read and understand this manual before using the equipment in order to ensure safe operation.
--[p9]-- 1.1 Thoroughly read the manual first!
Thoroughly read this manual. To prevent any severe injury or unexpected accident, the precautions provided in this manual must be read carefully before turning ON the power to the equipment. Any use of the equipment not complying with the terms of this manual will constitute serious fault by the operator for which we cannot be responsible.

===== [p10-10] WS-820/MTR series manuals > 1.7 Limitations (1块) =====

The equipment was designed and manufactured with special attention to safety considerations. However, it is impossible either to eliminate all potential sources of danger from such equipment or to anticipate all possible hazards and misuses. Therefore, it is critical that you familiarize yourself and all of your operators and other personnel with all of the noted precautions, and take maximum care when operating the equipment.

===== [p123-123] CAUTION > 4.3 Checking remaining lots (2块) =====
--[p123]-- 4.3 Checking remaining lots
1) First, visually confirm that none of the lots, cassettes, or tools remain in the equipment.
If anything remains in the equipment, proceed to step (2) below and perform home return and then remove the item manually.
2) Also visually conform that the front cover, straightening vane, gangway and other covers are properly attached.
3) Select [Lot Tracking] from the [Operation] menu to display the [Lot Tracking] window. Then confirm that no lot data remains.
--[p123]-- 4) If lot data remains, click the “Delete All” button. When the confirmation dialog which asks you to confirm deletion of the lot data appears, click the “OK” button.

• Before beginning the replacement, turn OFF the power source breaker of the equipment, lock the breaker out, and attach tags to the breaker so that power is not supplied to the equipment. The lock-out key must be kept by the authorized employee after locking the breaker.

• When the replacement is completed, close the maintenance cover and lock it with a key.
After locking, the key must be removed from the lock and kept by the manager.
• Wear appropriate protective gear.

===== [p54-54] WARNING > 2.4.1 Temperature control (1块) =====
--[p54]-- 2.4.1 Temperature control
The heat-up and temperature control of the solution in a processing
bath are performed by the in-line heater or the electric thermocontroller mounted on the circulation system. Some processing baths incorporate an immersion-type heater in addition to the in-line heater.
After all conditions required to start temperature control have been satisfied, control can be started by turning the temperature control switch (located next to the overhead operation panel) to ON.

===== [p165-165] 7.1 Operation check for the maintenance cover sensors Equipment mode > (2) LPD (1块) =====
--[p165]-- (2) LPD
1) From the maintenance area, remove the securing bolts for the LPD lower rear cover and remove the cover.
2) Visually check that there is no leakage in the module bottom vat.
3) Put a clean cloth wet with water on the sensing section of the leakage detector in the module bottom vat. Confirm that the corresponding leakage alarm is activated.
4) Remove the cloth from the detector and wipe the sensing section with a dry clean cloth.
(It may take a little while until the sensing section has dried.)
5) Reset the “\*\* leakage” error.
6) Reattach the cover and secure it with bolts.

===== [p105-105] WARNING > 5.1.1 Horizontal position check (1块) =====
--[p105]-- 5.1.1 Horizontal position check
At each stop position in the horizontal direction, positioning sensors
as well as a BCD code (generated by an array of four photosensors) are used to determine the current position.
After the unit stops, the four photosensors generate BCD codes (beam intercept patterns that differ for each position) that indicate the current position.

===== [p183-183] 6 Thermocouple replacement procedure (at exit of inline heater) > How to handle the replace parts (1块) =====
--[p183]-- How to handle the replace parts
The replaced parts should be disposed of as industrial wastes.
(1) Rinse the replaced parts to eliminate all remaining chemical.
(2) Dry the parts.
(3) Securely pack the parts so that any chemical remaining inside will not leak out.
(4) Write down the parts names, materials and chemicals used.
(5) If special ventilation is necessary for safety, request that a qualified specialist handle the parts as industrial wastes.
In cases where storage is necessary, keep them in a designated safe place.
Inline heater replacement procedure

===== [p76-76] WARNING > 2.7.1 Bath cleaning procedure (when “Chemical control” is ON) (1块) =====
--[p76]-- 2.7.1 Bath cleaning procedure (when “Chemical control” is ON)
When “Chemical control” is ON, the bath cleaning sequence is performed as follows in either the automatic or manual mode.
(i) Chemical drainage
The chemical is drained.
(ii) DI water supply
When completion of chemical drainage is confirmed, DI water supply to the processing bath starts. The supply stops when DI water overflows the inner bath and the designated DI water level sensor in the outer bath turns ON.
(iii) DI water drainage
The DI water is drained and the bath cleaning sequence is completed when the drainage is completed.

--[p105]-- 5 Transfer unit

The number of transfer units vary with the processes and number of units included in the equipment.

===== [p88-88] WARNING > 3.3.1 Alarm setting for the resistivity meter (1块) =====
--[p88]-- 3.3.1 Alarm setting for the resistivity meter
Resistivity is measured by a cell meter in contact with the bath’s DI water. The resistivity meter’s operation panel at the rear of the equipment displays the measured value and can be used to set the alarm value.
Pressing “ALM.1” displays the current alarm value. To change the value, keep pressing “↑” and “ALM.1” until the desired value is displayed. Pressing “CELL A°C” displays the temperature at the cell.

===== [p23-23] WARNING > 2.4.3 Areas where electrical charges remain even after an emergency stop switch is pressed. (1块) =====
--[p23]-- 2.4.3 Areas where electrical charges remain even after an emergency stop switch is pressed.
Even after the emergency stop switch is activated, some areas continue to carry dangerous electrical current. Never touch these areas even after the emergency stop switch has been activated.
Whenever you intend to perform electrical work, always be sure to turn OFF the main circuit breaker in the factory.
For details on areas where electrical charges remain even after an emergency stop switch is pressed, refer to Appendix O1.2.4.1.

===== [p80-80] WARNING > 2.9 Level replenishment (1块) =====
--[p80]-- 2.9 Level replenishment
The level replenishment function refills the chemical when the amount in the processing bath falls below the specified level.
When the specified chemical level sensor in the outer bath turns OFF for 10 consecutive seconds, level replenishment starts by opening the supply valve of the relevant measuring tank.
Replenishment continues until the designated level sensor turns ON and the supply valve closes 1 second later.

===== [p174-174] 3 Replacement of circulation filter (cartridge type) > Disposal of replaced parts (1块) =====
--[p174]-- Disposal of replaced parts
After replacement, clean the old parts and dispose of them as industrial waste.
1) Clean the old parts with water to remove residual chemicals.
2) Wipe any water from the parts.
3) Pack each part. Take care that nothing can leak from the packed part.
4) Write down the name and material of the part and the name of the chemical adhered to it.
5) When you want to dispose of the part, commission a special contractor to handle it as industrial waste.
When setting it aside, store and manage it in a specified and safe place.

===== [p76-76] WARNING > 2.7.3 Repeat operation of bath cleaning (1块) =====

===== [p13-13] WS-820/MTR series manuals > 1.10.2 When an interlock is activated (1块) =====
--[p13]-- 1.10.2 When an interlock is activated
When an interlock is activated, be sure to report it to the authorized personnel as soon as possible and follow his/her instructions.

===== [p105-105] WARNING > 5.1 Positioning operation (1块) =====
--[p105]-- 5.1 Positioning operation
After comparing the current address and destination address, the equipment positions the transfer unit by rotating the stepping motor (with a specified number of pulses) to achieve the desired movement. Locations at each stop position are determined by checking two conditions: the pulse difference and where the unit breaks the position photosensor light beams.

===== [p87-87] WARNING > 3.2.5 Transfer to/from the empty QDR, H/C-QDR bath (Option) (1块) =====
--[p87]-- 3.2.5 Transfer to/from the empty QDR, H/C-QDR bath (Option)
When lot transfer to/from the empty QDR or H/C-QDR bath is specified and then $" 0 "$ is set as the processing time, the specified lot transfer is performed.
Rinsing bath module

===== [p195-195] 11 Water saving type pneumatic valve flow rate adjustment procedure > Adjustment procedure (1块) =====
--[p195]-- Adjustment procedure
(1) Open the rear cover of the circulation section in which adjustment is to be performed. (For the CHCL module, the relevant cover is located at the front of the equipment.)
Do not turn or remove the pneumatic valve head while DI water is being supplied.

===== [p22-22] WS-820/MTR series manuals > 2.4.1 In case of emergency (1块) =====
--[p22]-- 2.4.1 In case of emergency
Immediately press an emergency stop switch if an abnormality occurs in the equipment or there is any danger to the operators. This directly shuts off the power supply to all moving parts and terminates operations in all modules. For information on current-carrying parts, refer to Appendix O1.2.4.1.

===== [p120-120] WARNING > 4.1.8 Checking completion of temperature control (1块) =====
--[p120]-- 4.1.8 Checking completion of temperature control
For baths with temperature control functions, check if control has been completed and that an overheat alarm has not occurred. If temperature control has been completed, the internal green lamp in the “TC/OH” switch should be on. If it is not, pressing the “TC/OH” switch will start it blinking and begin the temperature control. When temperature control is completed, the green lamp should finally light up.
However, if the “TC/OH” switch's internal red lamp remains on or blinking, report it to a system supervisor or manager.
Preparation before processing

===== [p132-132] CAUTION > 4 Daily inspection (1块) =====
--[p132]-- 4 Daily inspection

===== [p23-23] WARNING > 2.4.2 Function of emergency stop switches (1块) =====
--[p23]-- 2.4.2 Function of emergency stop switches
This equipment is provided with emergency stop switches that are used to halt the equipment in case of emergency.
Pressing one of the emergency stop switches activates a related magnetic switch and shuts the equipment's power circuit OFF.
For details, refer to Appendix O1.2.4.2.

===== [p248-248] 12 Automatic cover opening/closing speed adjustment procedure > (2) Delete (1块) =====
--[p248]-- (2) Delete
Used when canceling the lot processing on the stage.
Select the lots which you wish to delete and then click this button.
“Delete” cannot be executed unless the step pause is completed.

===== [p187-187] 8 Circulation pump replacement procedure > Tools required (1块) =====
--[p187]-- Tools required
Tools/accessories: Standard tools (screwdriver, wrench, nippers), crimping tool, tightening jig, footstool

===== [p195-195] 11 Water saving type pneumatic valve flow rate adjustment procedure > CAUTION (1块) =====
--[p195]-- CAUTION
When the replacement is completed, close the maintenance cover and lock it with a key.   
After locking, the key must be removed from the lock and kept by the manager.

===== [p167-167] DANGER > Restart procedure (1块) =====
--[p167]-- Restart procedure
(1) Check that no tools remain in the equipment. Reattach all maintenance covers (by securing them with their screws).
(2) Confirm that there are no low exhaust pressure errors.
(3) Turn ON all the temperature control switches.
(4) Turn ON the transfer unit power.
(5) Switch the equipment mode from manual to automatic.
Procedure of greasing

===== [p40-40] WARNING > 1.3 Parts disposal (1块) =====
--[p40]-- 1.3 Parts disposal
When replacing consumable parts, handle them according to relevant laws and regulations as well as local standards. Please contact a waste management specialist for disposal of these parts. Exercise caution with regard to the following points.
• The replaced parts should be sufficiently cleaned and disposed of as industrial wastes.
• Write down the materials of the parts and the names of the chemicals used.
• Pack each part carefully so that nothing can leak or be released from the package.

===== [p23-23] WARNING > 2.4.5 Cancellation of an emergency stop switch (1块) =====
--[p23]-- 2.4.5 Cancellation of an emergency stop switch
For the individual cancellation procedures of the emergency stop switches, refer to the manuals for each one and follow the instructions given in the manual. An operator should not cancel an emergency stop switch based on his/her own judgment.

===== [p32-32] WARNING > 2.12 Periodic inspection of terminal blocks and fittings (1块) =====
--[p32]-- 2.12 Periodic inspection of terminal blocks and fittings
• To prevent heat generation accidents due to loose connection on the terminal block or leakage accidents from loose piping fittings, periodic inspection and retightening must be performed.
• For details of the periodic inspection of terminal blocks and fittings, contact our nearest sales office, listed at the end of this manual.

===== [p14-14] WS-820/MTR series manuals > 1.11.3 Countermeasures against accident (1块) =====
--[p14]-- 1.11.3 Countermeasures against accident
When an accident with the chemical solution (i.e., physical contact) occurs, take appropriate measures by following the aid and release measures against chemical accidents noted on respective MSDS.
